Method: 将基准修订表述为在效用约束下的现实性改进：对基准数据进行确定性修订以提高现实性指标（缺失结构、简洁性、结构可行性、人口对齐），同时保证通过既定的运营效用下限。基准基于Synthea生成的患者和示范EHR工作流，比较基线、两种确定性修订和一个天真的密集化对照。

Result: 基线基准非常稀薄：采样对缺失率79.44%、仅12.75%行可操作、38.94%患者无可操作测量、前三token集中度达100%。两种确定性修订在不低于当前效用下限的前提下显著改善了上述现实性面板，而天真密集化保持了不现实的模板化特征。内部现实性与对汇总运营参考的源拟合是相关但不相同的目标。

Conclusion: 合成临床基准可以通过现有的效用检查但仍在结构上不现实，应将合成基准质量显式作为优化目标，并以效用作为约束而非充分证据。

Abstract: Synthetic clinical benchmarks for enterprise AI agents can pass existing utility checks and still remain structurally unrealistic, especially in privacy-sensitive healthcare settings where operational data are hard to access. We study how to improve such benchmarks without breaking the downstream utility checks already used in practice.
  We formulate benchmark revision as utility-constrained realism improvement: dataset changes should increase realism while staying above an operational utility floor. We instantiate this idea on a care-gap benchmark derived from Synthea-generated patients exercised through demonstration electronic health record workflows and then processed by the same downstream pipeline as operational data. Realism is measured through missingness structure, simplicity, structural plausibility, and population alignment.
  The baseline benchmark is extremely thin: sampled-pair missingness is 79.44%, only 12.75% of rows are actionable, 38.94% of patients have zero actionable measures, and top-three token concentration reaches 100.0%. Two deterministic revisions improve these panels while remaining above the current utility floor, whereas a naive densification control preserves unrealistic templating. We further show that internal benchmark realism and source fidelity to an aggregate operational reference are related but distinct objectives. These results suggest that synthetic benchmark quality should be optimized explicitly, with utility treated as one constraint rather than as sufficient evidence of realism.

</details>


### [94] [The Illusion of Visual Tool-Use: A Causal Audit of Thinking with Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06270)
*Zhiheng Wang,Bo Peng,Lai Wei,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用因果干预（策略/轨迹/步骤）衡量视觉工具调用对答案的因果贡献，发现多数调用并无实际效果，提出“视觉工具使用的幻觉”概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态 LLM 的主动视觉操作（如裁剪放大）在实践中常带来高代价但少量或无益的改进，作者旨在弄清这些返回的视觉证据是否真正因果影响模型答案，从而诊断与改进视觉工具使用的有效性。

Method: 构建将观测媒介路径与动作诱导捷径分离的因果图；通过三层干预（策略比较、轨迹观测破坏、步骤级反事实替换）来审计政策，并提出 Visual Evidence Gain 来量化单次观测的因果贡献；在多模型多基准上做实验，分析失败模式与增益分布。

Result: 这篇论文通过因果化的视角审计多模态大模型的视觉工具使用，发现所谓的“thinking-with-images”在整体上存在幻觉——虽然总体准确率有提升，但在大多数回合中视觉操作并未对答案产生因果效应，仅在一小部分“校准良好”的回合中才有效。论文提出了三层干预（策略、轨迹、步骤），并定义了步骤级估计量 Visual Evidence Gain 来量化每次返回观测的贡献，进而辨别两类失效模式：Calling Without Looking（调用但无因果影响）和 Looking Without Planning（观测有信息但调用时机混乱）。实证覆盖六个代表性模型和五个细粒度感知基准，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 尽管 aggregate accuracy 显示增益，但视觉工具使用在因果层面并不普遍有效，改进应聚焦于调用时机、策略校准与实际观测的有用性。

Abstract: The "thinking-with-images" paradigm equips multimodal LLMs with active visual operations such as crop-and-zoom. However, models using these operations often achieve only marginal or negative gains over direct inference at substantially higher token cost. They may also repeatedly crop irrelevant regions and fail on questions that direct inference answers correctly. We ask whether the returned visual evidence causally affects the answer. To answer this question, we formulate visual tool-use as a causal graph that separates observation-mediated paths from action-induced shortcuts. We then audit it through interventions at the three levels: policy (comparing tool-use with direct inference), trajectory (corrupting all observations during rollout), and step (counterfactually replacing one individual observation under a fixed prefix). Our step-level estimand, Visual Evidence Gain, isolates the contribution of each returned observation. Across six representative models and five fine-grained perception benchmarks, we uncover policy miscalibration with two failure modes. In Calling Without Looking, returned observations have no causal effect on the answer. In Looking Without Planning, observations are informative but the call schedule is incoherent. A trajectory-level diagnostic decomposes the policy-level accuracy gain and shows that the gain is concentrated in a Calibrated minority. We term this discrepancy the illusion of visual tool-use: despite aggregate accuracy gains, visual tool-use is not causally effective across a broad range of rollouts. The code is available at https://github.com/OpenCausaLab/CauAudit.

</details>


### [95] [QuanTiMedAI: Quantum-Enhanced Time-Series Model guided by Agentic AI for Cardiac Arrest Mortality Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06294)
*Mutasim Fuad Sarker,Adiba Rahman Namira,Wafa Binte Alam,Md Adnan Arefeen,Mahzabeen Emu,Sumaiya Tabassum Nimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QuanTiMedAI：用代理型LLM做临床特征发现，结合紧凑量子递归网络做时序死亡率预测，在MIMIC‑IV心脏骤停子集上实现了参数极少但性能超过经典基线的结果（AUROC=0.852，605参数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICU心脏骤停患者死亡率预测多依赖入院早期的静态汇总特征，忽视了整个ICU住院期间生理指标随时间变化的动态信息；本文提出一种将代理型大语言模型用于临床特征发现并结合紧凑量子递归网络以捕捉时序性的混合框架，以提升死亡率预测性能并降低参数规模。

Method: 构建代理型LLM进行临床知识驱动的特征发现与选择；设计并训练一个基于量子态/变分量子电路的紧凑量子递归网络以捕捉时间维度的非线性特征增强；在MIMIC‑IV心脏骤停患者子集上进行训练/验证/测试，并开展结构化消融研究评估每个组件的贡献。

Result: 基于MIMIC‑IV心脏骤停患者队列的实验证明：代理型LLM引导的特征选择优于传统方法；量子增强序列模型在仅605个参数下达到AUROC=0.852，较当前任务的最先进基线提升约2.9%；消融实验系统地验证了各设计组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 量子增强的时序建模在参数显著更少的情况下可以超越经典循环网络；代理型LLM能够提供有助于机器学习的临床特征，但需谨慎验证其临床合理性与可重复性。

Abstract: Cardiac arrest remains one of the most lethal conditions encountered in intensive care units. Despite the growing availability of electronic health record data, existing mortality prediction studies in this population largely depend on static summaries derived from early admission. Such approaches ignore the temporal progression of physiological deterioration and recovery that unfolds throughout a patient's ICU stay. To address this limitation, we introduce QuanTiMedAI, a quantum-agentic framework developed for cardiac arrest mortality prediction using agentic AI guided quantum enhancement time series model. The proposed system combines an agentic large language model (LLM) for clinically informed feature discovery with a compact quantum recurrent network for temporality aware mortality prediction. Our findings demonstrate that agentic LLM-guided feature selection consistently outperforms conventional feature selection approaches, and the proposed quantum architecture achieves competitive predictive performance through nonlinear feature enhancement while keeping the number of parameters very low. Through extensive experimentation on a MIMIC-IV cohort of cardiac arrest patients, QuanTiMedAI's quantum-enhanced architecture attains an AUROC of 0.852 using only 605 parameters, an improvement of approximately 2.9\% over a current state-of-the-art baseline for this task. A structured ablation study systematically validates the contribution of each architectural design choice. These results show that quantum-enhanced sequential modeling can exceed classical recurrent networks while using substantially fewer parameters.

</details>


### [96] [Bias Analysis of L2 Speaking Assessment Systems Using Concept Activation Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06300)
*Arya Labroo,Mengjie Qian,Kate Knill*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将CAV分析扩展到文本与语音-文本Transformer评分器，发现“能否恢复概念的线性方向”与“该概念是否影响评分”是两个不同的问题；SAE改善可分性但可能降低对评分的实际影响力，强调在自动口语评分公平性审计中需区分这两者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险的二语（L2）口语评分场景中，自动评分系统需保证给分依据是语言能力而非不相关的说话者属性（如母语或年龄）。Transformer基模型虽然提升了准确度，但黑盒表征使得公平性与可解释性分析更困难。论文旨在开发/评估工具以检测并量化模型是否对不必要属性产生偏见。

Method: 论文在两类神经评分器上推广了基于概念激活向量（CAV）的分析：一个基于文本的BERT评分器和一个基于Whisper的语音+文本多模态评分器。作者首先在若干层激活上训练线性分类器得到CAVs，然后用梯度基灵敏度度量（gradient-based sensitivity）衡量这些概念对最终分数的影响。此外，引入稀疏自编码器（SAE）：在稀疏潜空间中学习CAV并映回激活空间，评估是否能提高概念的线性可分性与解释性。

Result: 主要结果包括：1) 概念能否被CAV线性恢复依赖于被探测的层和架构；2) 不同架构对概念敏感性的度量差异显著；3) SAE可提高CAV的线性可恢复性，但通常会削弱原始激活空间中的灵敏度度量，尤其在低维层上更显著；4) 因此，仅靠能否训练出CAV并不能说明该概念影响模型输出，必须同时度量影响力。

Conclusion: 本文结论是：在基于Transformer的自动口语评分系统中，能否用线性方向（CAV）恢复出人类可解释概念主要取决于被探测的表示与网络架构，而非概念本身；稀疏自编码器（SAE）能提高概念的线性可分性，但同时会削弱原始激活空间中该概念对预测分数的梯度敏感性，尤其在低维层中更明显；因此在审计偏差时必须区分“概念可恢复性（recoverability）”和“概念影响力（influence）”。

Abstract: Automatic speaking assessment systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings to mark second language (L2) learners' speaking tests, making it critical to show that their scores depend on speaking proficiency rather than irrelevant speaker attributes such as first language (L1) or age. Transformer-based foundation models have improved the accuracy of these L2 speaking graders, but their black-box representations make fairness and interpretability analysis more difficult. Building on prior work that used Concept Activation Vectors (CAVs) to detect bias towards unwanted attributes (`concepts') in feature-based graders, we extend CAV-based analysis to two neural speaking assessment systems: a text-based BERT grader and a speech-and-text multimodal grader based on Whisper. CAVs represent human-interpretable concepts as directions in a model's activation space, allowing us to distinguish between whether a concept is encoded in a model's internal representations and whether it influences the predicted score, the latter quantified using a gradient-based sensitivity metric. Since CAVs rely on linear separability, which is less likely in complex neural embedding spaces, we also investigate whether sparse autoencoders (SAEs) provide cleaner concept directions by learning CAVs in a sparse latent space and mapping them back to activation space. Our analysis shows that concept recoverability depends strongly on the representation and architecture being probed, rather than on the concept alone. Sensitivity to concepts is also architecture-dependent. SAEs make concepts more linearly recoverable, but attenuate the original activation-space sensitivity, especially in low-dimensional layers. These findings highlight the need to distinguish concept recoverability from concept influence when auditing bias in speaking assessment systems.

</details>


### [97] [HarnessOpt-Bench: Evaluating LLMs at Harness Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06301)
*Varun Ursekar,Apaar Shanker,Yash Maurya,Shehab Yasser,Vijay S. Kalmath,Veronica Chatrath,Yuan Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了HarnessOpt-Bench——一个用于评估在昂贵且有噪声的评估环境中，LLM作为“优化器”对代理harness进行端到端自动优化的基准。优化器在固定预算和受限测试分区下迭代修改harness并提交最终候选，由可信执行环境保证评估边界与审计。基准在4个下游任务上对5种前沿LLM进行了111次得分实验，发现优化器模型间差异大于其所用编码harness差异、原生harness并不总是更好、且收益随任务与种子设置显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被嵌入到更复杂的agentic系统中，整体性能受“harness”（提示、工具、控制流、内存与编排代码）影响甚大。自动化优化harness既是提升系统性能的重要路径，也是对模型自身能力的高阶挑战，但缺乏统一的测评协议来量化和比较模型在此任务上的表现。

Method: 设计了一个黑箱但受控的评测流程：优化器（LLM+编码harness）获取种子harness、分级评价反馈和固定评估预算，在受限环境下迭代编辑并提交最终候选；最终评分为在未访问的测试分区上相对于种子的归一化增益。使用可信执行环境（TEE）强制评估边界、计量资源并保存候选以便审计。对5个前沿LLM分别在共享编码harness和它们的原生harness上，针对4个下游任务、共111次带分数的运行进行了评估。

Result: 实验显示：1) 优化器模型间表现差异显著，优于因所用编码harness差异带来的影响；2) 原生harness并不总比共享编码harness强；3) 在不同任务与不同种子质量下，优化收益波动很大，表明任务与初始条件高度决定最终效果；4) 整体上仍有大量可改进空间，证明基准的辨别力与实用价值。

Conclusion: HarnessOpt-Bench成功将“自动化harness优化”定义为可测量、可区分的能力，并显示当前模型在这项任务上还有显著改进空间；基准为比较优化策略、衡量成本-收益与推动改进提供了通用协议。

Abstract: As LLMs are increasingly deployed within agentic systems, their capabilities depend not only on the model weights but also on the harness: the prompts, tools, control flow, memory, and orchestration code surrounding them. This makes automated harness optimization -- the iterative and evaluation-guided improvement of a harness by an AI system -- both an important route to improving AI systems and a demanding capability for AI systems themselves. Yet the community lacks a common protocol for measuring how well frontier LLMs perform at this task. We introduce HarnessOpt-Bench, a benchmark for end-to-end harness optimization under expensive and stochastic evaluation. An optimizer, an LLM paired with a coding harness, receives a target agent's seed harness, graded evaluation feedback, and a fixed target-evaluation budget. It edits the harness and nominates a final candidate, which is scored by its normalized gain over the seed on a held-out test partition that remains inaccessible throughout search. A trusted execution environment enforces the evaluation boundary, meters target-agent resource use, and preserves candidate versions for audit. We evaluate 5 frontier LLMs as optimizers both under a shared coding harness and under their native harnesses across 4 downstream tasks, over 111 scored runs. Experiment results show that optimizer models separate more than the coding harnesses they act through, native harnesses are not consistently superior, and gains vary substantially across tasks and seed regimes. These results establish harness optimization as a measurable and discriminative capability with large space for improvement.

</details>


### [98] [Beyond Top-K: Replacing Black-Box Retrieval with Interpretable Agentic Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06305)
*Sagar Tamang,Ayush Vyas,Tabarakul Hazarika*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Chunk+embed RAG fails on tables/financial docs. READ uses embedding-free agentic access to raw doc structure and wins substantially over dense retrieval (not over BM25).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing RAG pipelines using chunking+embeddings fail on structured numerical documents (financials) because chunks separate numbers from units/headers and many near-identical numeric cells confuse embedding spaces

Method: analysis of retrieval for long financial documents

Result: Proposed READ: an embedding-free, agentic but deterministic document search using normalized lexical search, structural navigation, bounded span reads over MCDP; on 51 questions READ answers 58.8% vs dense retrieval 15.7% (stat. sig.), BM25 indistinguishable from READ

Conclusion: Embedding-free, structure-aware retrieval is more reliable for long financial documents; agentic loop matters but embeddings are the main weakness here.

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation over long documents is dominated by one design: chunk the text, embed the chunks, and surface the top-k nearest neighbours of the query. We argue that for an important class of documents -- financial statements, audit reports, regulatory returns -- this design is structurally unsound, and we make the argument measurable. On a 780-page government financial report, 86.8% of content lines are table rows, thousands of near-identical figures compete in one embedding space, and a figure inherits its unit from a header a median of 13 lines above it -- so a chunk boundary routinely separates a number from whether it is in lakh or crore, an error of two orders of magnitude. A table-aware chunker built as a steelman fixes the unit problem but leaves 27-30% of numeric chunks with no fiscal-year header at every chunk size we tried. We propose READ (Reliable Embedding-free Agentic Document-search), in which an agent reads the raw document through three deterministic operations -- normalized lexical search, structural navigation, and bounded span reads -- exposed over the Model Context Protocol, so a trajectory is a replayable audit trail, not an opaque similarity score. On 51 verified questions READ answers 58.8% against dense retrieval's 15.7% (p_Holm = 2 x 10^-5) -- or 35.3% tuned, which READ still leads by 23.5 points (p_Holm = 0.017). An agent given the same loop but a top-k tool reaches only 27.5%, locating the gain in the interface rather than in iteration. We also report what the evidence does not support: BM25 is statistically indistinguishable from READ, so our result separates embedding-based from embedding-free retrieval, not agentic from lexical search.

</details>


### [99] [TRAJDEBUG: Tracing Error Lifecycle to Identify Critical Failures in Long-Horizon Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06346)
*Yunjia Qi,Zehua Yin,Xintong Shi,Hao Peng,Songyuanyi Lu,Yixian Liu,Richeng Xuan,Yuhong Liu,Zhichao Hu,Xiaozhi Wang,Lei Hou,Bin Xu,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TrajDebug，一个追踪错误生命周期以定位失败轨迹中首个关键错误的框架，并配套构建了含486条标注轨迹的TrajErrBench，实验表明其在多种LLM代理基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在复杂任务中表现出能力但易发生层叠错误且难以调试，尤其是：证据分散的长轨迹使单步判别困难，以及失败轨迹中多处局部错误只有部分最终导致失败。

Method: 采用多粒度历史压缩降低长序列干扰，基于证据的步级错误判别以整合分散证据，并对每个错误追踪其是否被后续步骤解决及其终端影响以实现关键归因。

Result: 在来自Tau2Bench和SWE-Bench Pro的486条人工标注失败轨迹上，TrajDebug在错误发现和关键归因上优于现有基线，且其诊断能为修复策略提供有效指导；作者计划开源代码与数据。

Conclusion: TrajDebug能在长轨迹和多局部错误场景下更准确地发现并归因关键错误，其诊断结果能为改进下游代理性能提供可操作的反馈。

Abstract: LLM-based agentic systems have shown remarkable capabilities in complex domains, while suffering from cascading errors and difficulty in debugging. Critical error detection aims to locate the earliest error step in a failed trajectory that is responsible for the final failure. However, progress faces two main challenges. First, long trajectories make it difficult to identify individual errors, since the evidence for judging a step may be scattered across distant instructions, observations, and prior context. Second, failed trajectories often contain multiple local errors with different downstream effects, only some of which remain responsible for the final failure. In this work, we propose TrajDebug, an error-lifecycle tracing framework that addresses long-trajectory error discovery with multi-granularity history compression and evidence-based error identification, and supports critical attribution by tracing each error's resolution status and terminal impact. We further construct TrajErrBench, a benchmark of 486 manually annotated failed trajectories from Tau2Bench and SWE-Bench Pro, covering realistic tool-use and coding scenarios. Experiments across diverse agent benchmarks show that TrajDebug achieves the best overall performance over existing baselines, and application studies further demonstrate that its diagnoses provide actionable feedback for improving downstream agent success. We will release the codes and data to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [100] [Challenges in Evaluating Explanation Methods for Static and Evolving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06351)
*Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出当前XAI评估不足，提供人类实验示例并探索对流数据中解释的自适应方法（包括反事实），并讨论跟踪数据/模型/解释共演化的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前XAI评估不充分，尤其在持续变化数据流与偏差修正（概念去学习）场景下，迫切需要可行的评估与自适应解释方法。

Method: 使用DetoxAI为案例系统，进行人类主体评估实验，开发并试验反事实适应方法以应对概念漂移，提出跟踪共演化的讨论性框架。

Result: 提出并评估了在图像分类解释性AI中进行基于人类的评估、处理概念漂移以自适应解释、并讨论了使用反事实解释的经验，结合DetoxAI系统演示偏差检测与概念去学习，强调了数据、模型与解释的共进化挑战。

Conclusion: 需要综合化评估框架、持续化评估与解释版本管理，并以可复现实验与定量指标验证适应性与可靠性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the limitations of Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) with respect to insufficient evaluation. They are illustrated through the DetoxAI image recognition system for bias detection and concept unlearning. Then, an example of a human-grounded evaluation of methods for explaining image classification is presented. The paper further explores methods for adapting explanations to evolving data streams with concept drift. Experiences with adapting counterfactuals for this problem are discussed. Finally it is related to the challenges of tracking the co-evolution of data, models, and explanations.\footnote{This paper has been accepted for a publication in J.Nalepa (ed) Explainable AI in Space. Proceedings of EASi 2026 Workshop at IJCAI-ECAI 2026 Bremen, Springer CCIS vol 3107 (2016).}

</details>


### [101] [The Low Frequency Trap: Video Language Models Fail at Simple Event Bookkeeping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06361)
*Sarvesh Baskar,Zikui Cai,Shayan Shabihi,Anirudh Satheesh,Muhammad R. Islam,Udari Madhushani Sehwag,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 引入基于可执行轨迹的参数化视频事件计数评估，揭示事件表征与时间分辨率主导的失败模式，并提供细粒度诊断替代单一汇总准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界视频基准难以解耦影响计数性能的因素（事件数、频率、时长、视觉复杂度），程序化基准虽然可控但通常只评估最终答案，缺乏与可执行真值轨迹的逐事件对齐审计。作者旨在桥接二者，给出细粒度的时间对齐评估框架。

Method: 构造三类受控视频任务（弹跳球接触、视觉眨眼、类别状态变换），在2190个视频中系统化变动事件数N和频率F，保持渲染不变，并为每个视频提供可执行的事件时间轨迹以进行逐时间戳比对；评估包括最终计数准确率与事件恢复率，并研究采样率与提示策略的影响。

Result: 发现分阶段的时间失败模式：模型对持续性状态变换在低频低计数下有一个可靠区间（例如Gemini 3.6 Flash在0.5–1.0 Hz下可可靠计数到12次），但对瞬时眨眼无可靠正计数区；在高数目高频率下，仅0.2%最终计数完全正确，事件恢复率仅18.1%；提高采样率能提升总分数（例如Bounce Ball从19.6%提到29.3%）但序列一致性仍极低（仅3.7%），提示额外帧可以改善表面指标但不保证真实事件恢复。不同提示策略收效有限，真实世界视频也呈现出类似在低计数集中成功的模式。

Conclusion: 作者提出了可执行事件轨迹驱动的参数化剖面（trace-grounded parametric profiling），展示视频事件计数的失败主要来源于事件表征（瞬时 vs 持续）和时间分辨率，且在高频高计数情形下模型几乎无法恢复真实事件序列。

Abstract: Real-world video benchmarks provide broad coverage, but their fixed clips entangle event count, rate, duration, and visual complexity, making failure modes hard to isolate. While existing programmatic benchmarks offer better control, they score only the final answer rather than auditing reported events against executable ground truth. To bridge this gap, we introduce trace-grounded parametric profiling for event counting in three controlled video tasks: bouncing-ball wall contacts, visual blinks, and categorical state transitions. Across 2,190 videos, we vary event count N and frequency F while holding rendering fixed. Each video includes an executable event trace for capability-surface estimation and timestamp-level evaluation. Our results reveal a staged temporal failure. At an 80% reliability threshold, Gemini 3.6 Flash reliably counts persistent state transitions up to 12 events at 0.5 and 1.0 Hz, yet demonstrates no reliable positive-count region for transient blinking events. Thus, event representation dictates whether a model initially accesses evidence -- a limitation that compounds as count and frequency increase. In the high-count, high-frequency regime, only 0.2% of final counts are correct and the model recovers just 18.1% of true events. To test if visual access is the primary bottleneck, we increase sampling rate. Although this boosts Bounce Ball accuracy from 19.6% to 29.3%, the reported sequence agrees with ground truth only 3.7% of the time. Extra frames can therefore inflate final scores without producing faithful event recovery. Different prompting strategies yield similarly limited gains, and real-world video evaluations show the same concentration of success at low event counts. Ultimately, trace-grounded profiling shifts video evaluation from aggregate accuracy metrics to a detailed diagnostic of where temporal reasoning fails.

</details>


### [102] [Tracing the Heart: An Evidence-Linked Pipeline for Heart-Failure Feature Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06366)
*Soorya Ram Shimgekar,Michelle Hu,Dorisa Shehi,Daniel Kang,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Koustuv Saha,Christian Poellabauer,Christopher Lee,Sajeev Singh,Piyum Zonooz,Navin Kumar,Zeeshan Ahmed,Priyadarshini Kachroo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: nMAS is a rubric-grounded, evidence-linked multi-agent pipeline that automates heart-failure feature engineering; it produced many auditable features and substantially improved phenotyping AUROC on a 500-record test, but evaluation was single-institution and used dummy records, so external validation is needed.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Automate and make auditable the labor-intensive EHR feature engineering for heart failure by integrating guideline-based clinical reasoning and evidence provenance, addressing limitations of rule-based and LLM-only methods.

Method: A multi-agent pipeline (nMAS) that maps nine EHR source tables to clinical features using rubric-grounded rules and evidence-linking, generates structured and aggregated features, verifies integrity and provenance, and is audited by a restricted LLM; evaluation used 500 dummy patient records and held-out AUROC for HFrEF and HFpEF phenotyping, plus independent LLM-based rubric scoring.

Result: Developed Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS) that produced 132 structured and 70 rubric-scored aggregated features from 500 dummy patient records across nine EHR tables; features passed structural, rubric, and provenance checks and were audited by a restricted LLM. Adding aggregated features improved held-out AUROC from 0.895 to 0.963 for HFrEF and 0.870 to 0.910 for HFpEF. Independent LLM rubric assessment scored features at 81.5% of maximum.

Conclusion: Automated, auditable feature engineering for complex cardiovascular EHR data is feasible and can substantially enhance phenotyping performance, but current evaluation is limited and further external validation, robustness testing, and human review are required.

Abstract: Electronic health record (EHR) feature engineering is a major bottleneck in clinical research and AI, accounting for 39-45% of data scientists' workload. This is especially pronounced in heart failure, which affects an estimated 6.7 million U.S. adults and requires integrating fragmented EHR data with disease-specific, guideline-based clinical reasoning. Existing rule-based and large language model (LLM)-based approaches offer only partial automation with limited maintainability and evidence traceability. We developed the Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS), an evidence-linked, rubric-grounded pipeline for automated heart-failure feature engineering, and evaluated it on 500 dummy patient records from nine EHR source tables. nMAS generated 132 structured and 70 rubric-scored aggregated features, verified for structural integrity, rubric compliance, and provenance, and audited by a restricted LLM. Adding the aggregated features improved held-out AUROC from 0.895 to 0.963 for HFrEF and 0.870 to 0.910 for HFpEF phenotyping, and an independent LLM-based rubric assessment of evidence support and methodological soundness scored the features at 81.5% of maximum points. These results demonstrate the feasibility of automated, auditable feature engineering for complex cardiovascular EHR data, though evaluation was limited to a single-institution cohort and external validation is needed.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Towards a Risk Assessment of Malicious Skill Files in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05223)
*Rui Yang,Michael Fu,Kla Tantithamthavorn,Chetan Arora,Joey Chua*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Natural-language skill interfaces let malicious shell commands hide inside benign-looking skills; synthesized adversarial skills reliably exploit enterprise coding agents at scale; evaluation pipeline and benchmark are provided for reproducible assessment.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Autonomous coding agents load external skill files to specialize behavior, which expands attack surface by allowing malicious shell commands to be embedded inside natural-language skills that agents will execute with delegated authority. The paper aims to quantify and demonstrate this risk and provide a benchmark and evaluation methodology.

Method: Adversarial skill-synthesis using six LLMs across four model families to convert 471 real-world shell commands into benign-appearing natural-language skill files; creation of a benchmark of 2,826 skills mapped to 11 MITRE ATT&CK tactics; reproducible evaluation pipeline combining run stratification, evidence anchoring, a refusal veto, and deterministic declared-intent override, adjudicated by a three-judge LLM panel validated against a blind human gold standard (Cohen's kappa = 0.85); large-scale empirical runs (5,629) against two enterprise agents (Gemini CLI and Qwen Code).

Result: Generated a benchmark of 2,826 adversarial skills from 471 commands mapped to MITRE tactics; evaluation showed Gemini CLI was exploited in 95.5–96.1% of runs and Qwen Code in 71.6–74.0% (range from raw majority vote to declared-intent-corrected estimate) across 5,629 runs; explicit safety recognition by agents occurred in only 1.99% of runs. The LLM-judge panel showed high agreement with human labels (Cohen's kappa = 0.85).

Conclusion: Skill-interface risk is a substantial, model-agnostic threat to enterprise coding agents. Enterprises should assess and mitigate risk (vetting, least privilege, runtime controls) before adopting such agents. The paper releases code and dataset for community use.

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents are increasingly embedded in enterprise software workflows with delegated authority over connected systems. Central to this architecture is the agent skills interface: folders of instructions and scripts that agents load dynamically to specialize their behavior. This interface also widens the attack surface, letting malicious shell commands hide within natural-language skill files. We make three contributions. First, an adversarial skill-synthesis method using six LLMs across four families to transform 471 real-world shell commands into benign-appearing skills, released as a benchmark of 2,826 skills mapped to 11 MITRE ATT&CK tactics. Second, a reproducible evaluation pipeline coupling run stratification, evidence anchoring, a refusal veto, and a deterministic declared-intent override with a three-judge LLM-as-a-judge panel, validated against a blind human gold standard (Cohen's kappa = 0.85). Third, a large-scale characterization of two enterprise-grade agents across 5,629 completed runs. Gemini CLI is exploited in 95.5-96.1% of runs and Qwen Code in 71.6-74.0% (raw majority vote to declared-intent-corrected estimate, both within the human gold standard), nearly invariant to the generating model. Explicit safety recognition occurs in only 1.99% of runs. Enterprises must assess and mitigate skill-interface risk before adopting coding agents. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/awsm-research/AgentJailbreak

</details>


### [104] [Improving Debugging in Verification-Aware Languages Through Automated Fault Localization: A Case Study in Dafny](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05399)
*Álvaro Silva,Isabel Amaral,João Pascoal Faria,Alexandra Mendes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文比较了两种用于验证感知语言（以Dafny为例）的自动故障定位范式：基于状态的快照式定位（借鉴AutoFix）和基于反例的多层次定位（从原始反例到结构化单路径排序再到多路径聚合）。在MutDafny生成的变异数据集上，基于反例的方法总体上显著优于基于状态的方法；在单条路径上做结构化排序带来最大提升，多路径聚合增加了稳健性并减少求解器路径偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dafny等验证感知语言将规范嵌入源代码，但当验证失败时，反馈通常仅指示违反的条件或一条失败路径，开发者需要手动在长轨迹中定位根本原因；因此研究如何自动定位根本故障以节省调试成本。

Method: 实现了两类定位器：①基于状态的定位器：通过推断不变量和谓词，复制AutoFix的“快照”思想来发现可疑状态；②基于反例的定位器族：逐步增强地使用验证器输出——原始反例提取、对单条失败执行轨迹的结构化排名、以及对多条反例轨迹的聚合。评估使用MutDafny对DafnyBench进行变异生成并用EXAM分数衡量定位效果。

Result: 在所构造的变异基准上，基于反例的方案优于基于状态的方案；结构化单轨迹排序带来最大改进，多轨迹聚合进一步提升覆盖率并缓解求解器路径偏差，证明了反例的使用方式与多样性对定位效果的关键作用。

Conclusion: 有效的故障定位需要利用验证器产生的反例信息，并且对这些反例进行合理的结构化和多样化处理；纯粹的状态快照/不依赖反例的方法在该场景中性能明显较差。

Abstract: Verification-aware languages, like Dafny, integrate formal specifications directly into source code to enable static correctness checks. However, when verification fails, the feedback provided is often limited to the specific condition of the error, such as a violated postcondition, rather than the root cause of the fault. While Dafny's counterexample features provide concrete execution traces, these typically expose a single failing path per assertion failure, leaving the developer to manually look through the entire trace to locate the error.
  This paper investigates automated fault localization for verification-aware languages by comparing two paradigms: state-based and counterexample-based localization. Our state-based localization strategy replicates the ``snapshot'' methodology of AutoFix by inferring invariants and predicates to identify suspicious program states. The counterexample-based strategy consists of a family of techniques that progressively enrich the use of verifier output: from raw counterexample extraction, to structured single-trace ranking, and to multi-trace aggregation.
  To validate these methods, we present an evaluation framework using MutDafny to generate a diverse mutant dataset from DafnyBench and measure localization effectiveness using the EXAM score. Our results show that counterexample-based approaches substantially outperform state-based localization in this setting. Structured ranking over a single trace yields the largest improvement over raw counterexample output, while multi-trace aggregation provides additional gains in robustness and debugging utility by increasing coverage and reducing path bias introduced by the solver. These findings demonstrate that effective fault localization in verification-aware languages depends both on using counterexample information, and how that information is structured and diversified.

</details>


### [105] [Reasoning from Traces: Divergence-Guided Agentic Repair of WebAssembly Discrepancies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05521)
*Liyan Huang,Kaicheng Wang,Weihang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: WasmMend通过差分跟踪定位分歧并用LLM生成补丁，能较高成功率地自动修复Native–Wasm功能性差异（70%），优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨编译将C/C++生成Wasm与本地二进制常出现运行时差异，根因通常位于平台级运行时或编译器实现，源码层面难以直接定位或修复，现有LLM修复代理难以应对这种隐藏的运行时差异。

Method: 两阶段流程：1) 差分执行跟踪分析（differential trace analysis）定位首次产生分歧的函数；2) 基于定位结果，使用LLM代理推理根因并生成修补补丁，以消除执行分歧。

Result: 在真实世界C/C++项目上的实验，WasmMend达成70.0%修复率；对比基线：无引导的agentic方法50.2%，以及带修复时LLM插桩的方法54.5%，表明基于分歧定位的推理显著提升修复效果。

Conclusion: WasmMend能自动修复本地与Wasm二进制之间的功能性差异，通过差异引导的定位加上LLM生成补丁的方法，在真实C/C++项目上取得了显著效果（70.0%修复率），优于两种基线方法。

Abstract: WebAssembly (Wasm) promises seamless reuse of C/C++ codebases as portable, fast, sandboxed binaries. In practice, however, this promise often falls short: recent studies show that cross-compiling the same C/C++ source to Wasm and native binaries frequently leads to runtime discrepancies, owing to library implementation differences or compiler bugs. Since the root causes lie in the platform-level runtime and are hidden beneath the source code, even state-of-the-art LLM-based repair agents often fail to fix these discrepancies. In this paper, we present WasmMend, the first system to automatically repair Native-Wasm functional discrepancies. WasmMend converts the undirected exploration to a focused reasoning task in two stages: First, a novel differential trace analysis approach localizes the function where Wasm and native executions initially diverge; guided by this localization, LLM agents then reason about the root causes and generate patches that eliminate the divergent behavior. Experiments on real-world C/C++ projects show that WasmMend achieves a fix rate of 70.0%, compared to 50.2% for the agentic baseline and 54.5\% for the approach augmented with repair-time LLM-based instrumentation, demonstrating the value of divergence-guided reasoning for cross-platform repair.

</details>


### [106] [Exploring Dependence, Overreliance, and Addiction Related Behaviors Associated with Large Language Model Use Among Software Engineers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05561)
*Ronnie de Souza Santos,Italo Santos,Matheus de Moraes Leça,Cleyton Magalhaes,Mairieli Wessel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 119-practitioner survey: LLMs are habitually used and functionally integrated; overreliance exists but verification remains common; addiction-like issues are rare and mild. Emphasize trust calibration and verification.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs are increasingly integrated into developers' workflows, prompting concerns about developers' dependence, overreliance, and addiction-like behaviors and the implications for software quality and professional judgment.

Method: Exploratory cross-sectional survey of 119 software practitioners. Analysis used descriptive statistics for quantitative items and qualitative thematic analysis on open-ended responses.

Result: Participants primarily report functional dependence on LLMs in professional software engineering: LLMs are integrated into routine tasks due to productivity/efficiency gains. Evidence of overreliance appears where developers prioritize LLM outputs over documentation or peer input, yet most continue to verify outputs. Addiction-related behaviors are less frequent and manifest as difficulty moderating use or emotional attachment rather than severe impaired control. Overall, LLMs are becoming habitual tools; authors recommend promoting appropriate reliance via trust calibration, professional judgment, and verification.

Conclusion: LLMs have become habitual components of professional software development. Most usage is functional, but there is risk of overreliance; mitigation should focus on supporting calibrated trust, professional judgment, and systematic verification to avoid inappropriate dependence.

Abstract: The widespread adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) has changed how software engineers perform everyday development activities. While these systems provide substantial support for tasks such as code generation, debugging, and documentation, their increasing integration into professional workflows has also raised questions regarding developers' reliance on these tools and the emergence of dependence, overreliance, and addiction-related behaviors. This study investigates how software engineers experience the use of LLMs during professional software development, with attention to behavioral patterns associated with dependence, overreliance, and addiction-related behaviors. An exploratory survey was conducted with 119 software practitioners. The data were analyzed using descriptive statistics and qualitative thematic analysis of participants' open-ended responses. Participants primarily described functional dependence, with LLMs becoming integrated into routine software engineering activities because of the productivity and efficiency they provide. Responses also suggested patterns consistent with overreliance, particularly through prioritizing LLMs over documentation or peer consultation while continuing to verify generated outputs. Reports associated with addiction-related behaviors were less common and primarily reflected difficulty moderating use or emotional attachment to the technology rather than impaired control. The findings suggest that LLMs are becoming a habitual component of professional software engineering practice. While most reported use appears functional, the results indicate the importance of promoting appropriate reliance by supporting trust calibration, professional judgment, and verification throughout software development.

</details>


### [107] [JTA: Joint Testability Architecture for Scenario-Based Validation of Safety-Critical Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05594)
*Wenyao Xue,Jiandi Wang,Yichen Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: JTA提出了面向场景的架构化可测试性分析框架，强调控制、观测与隔离三要素及其桥接，提供场景合同与盲点识别，示例性评估表明部分场景成熟度差异明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键软件验证不仅依赖被测系统，还需构造受控场景、将执行证据整理为判定级别并能够将异常归因到可行动因。现有研究多侧重工件，缺乏架构层面支持联合能力的推理与设计，故提出JTA作为补充。

Method: 提出架构化概念模型：三要素合一的对象、三维能力（可控/可观/可隔）、三域/三桥的组织结构及分析-设计-评估-改进闭环；引入场景合同、联合能力评估与盲点识别，最后以ArduPilot避险验证为案例进行示范性分析。

Result: 论文提出联合可测试性架构（JTA），将测试场景、测试系统与被测系统作为单一分析与设计对象，沿可控性、可观测性、可隔离性三维度组织，通过三域、三桥与分析-设计-评估-改善闭环支持场景合同、联合能力评估与盲点识别。以ArduPilot避险验证为示例，指出链路丢失场景较成熟，而状态估计异常在证据对齐与归因语义上更难验证。JTA定位为现有测试/安全技术的体系化补充而非替代。

Conclusion: JTA在概念上填补了以工件为中心的测试性研究空白，优势在于提供跨系统的结构化思考路径与可落地的改进方向。要提升影响力需补强形式化度量、工具化支持和更广泛的实证验证（跨系统、多场景、定量评估），并明确与现行安全流程/标准的整合策略。

Abstract: Validation adequacy in safety-critical software depends on more than the system under test. Critical scenarios must be constructed under controlled conditions, execution evidence must be aligned into verdict-ready form, and abnormal outcomes must be attributable to actionable causes. Existing testability research remains largely artifact-centric and offers little architectural support for reasoning about the combined capability of the scenario, the test system, and the system under test. Joint Testability Architecture (JTA) addresses this gap by treating those three elements as a single object of analysis and design. It characterizes validation capability along three dimensions--controllability, observability, and isolability--and organizes them through three domains, three bridges, and an analysis-design-evaluation-refinement loop. JTA also introduces scenario contracts, joint capability assessment, validation blind-spot identification, and bridge-oriented design actions that map capability gaps to concrete improvements in control points, evidence organization, and attribution boundaries. An illustrative analysis of ArduPilot failsafe validation shows that link-loss scenarios are comparatively mature, whereas state-estimation anomaly scenarios remain harder to validate because evidence alignment and attribution semantics are weaker. JTA is not a replacement for existing testing or safety-analysis techniques; it provides an architectural basis for modeling, designing, and assessing scenario-based validation in safety-critical software.

</details>


### [108] [Towards Competence-Based Management for Open Source Software Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05599)
*Sabahat Younas,Márcia Moraes,Fabio Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过提取贡献的源代码度量并训练能力预测模型，作者旨在更精确地评估开源贡献者的能力，以便识别和培养可替代核心贡献者的人选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心贡献者离职导致开源项目风险增加，需要快速识别、招募和培养新贡献者以维持项目健康。现有研究未能深入量化贡献者能力水平，导致对候选人能力判断不足。

Method: 从贡献的源代码中收集代码度量（未在摘要中罗列具体度量项），并基于这些度量构建并训练一个能力预测模型，用于估计解决任务所需的能力等级。

Result: 提出了一个基于贡献代码的度量体系与能力模型，用以预测完成任务所需的能力等级；并发布了包含代码、数据和文档的复现包（DOI:10.5281/zenodo.21605122）。

Conclusion: 精确评估贡献者能力能帮助项目识别潜在替补并有针对性地培训，从而缓解核心贡献者流失风险。作者提供了可复现的数据和代码支持进一步研究。

Abstract: Contributors to Open Source Software (OSS) projects are vital to maintaining the health of both communities and projects. However, the number of projects experiencing core contributors' disengagement has increased to the point that it risks the projects' survival. Finding new contributors to replace the workforce is challenging and time-intensive due to several factors, including a lack of precise knowledge about potential candidates' competences, which may need to be confirmed through interviews and exams. Previous studies provided indications of the contributor's competences, although they lack depth in understanding competence levels, which can result in poor knowledge about the contributor's capabilities. To address this gap, we assess contributors' competence by collecting code metrics related to source code from contributions. We also propose a competence model able to predict the competence level required to solve tasks. By properly assessing contributors' competences, we can identify and train candidates to replace core contributors. Our replication package, including code, data, and documentation, is available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21605122

</details>


### [109] [Keeping Models and Code in Sync: Roundtrip Engineering for Tactical Domain-Driven Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05612)
*Weixing Zhang,Mario Herb,Wai Chung Dorothy Cheng,Michael Wagner,Bowen Jiang,Tianhai Liu,Anne Koziolek*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: JDomInO 通过共享元模型实现 Java 代码与领域模型的双向同步，前向路径已在完整示例上验证，反向路径在做单元级验证，目标是减少模型与代码的漂移并为 AI 助手提供语义上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 域驱动设计（DDD）提供共享词汇，但模型与代码随演化会发生偏离，团队缺乏可自动保持两者一致的工具。

Method: 使用共享元模型作为单一事实源；实现确定性模型->代码生成器；实现代码->模型的逆向映射；对关键映射逻辑编写单元测试；在酒店管理用例上进行前向端到端验证；提出将生成的域模型供 AI 助手使用以强化语义边界与聚合约束。

Result: 提出 JDomInO：基于共享元模型的双向同步工具链。前向：从域模型确定性生成 Java 代码结构（已在酒店管理场景上覆盖元模型全部12类构建块并完成验证）。反向：从现有 Java 源码重建域模型（映射逻辑已通过单元测试，端到端验证进行中）。并提出将结构化域模型作为 AI 代码助手的精确上下文层。

Conclusion: 方法具有实际价值与可操作性，但当前验证仍不完整。需要补充端到端一致性度量、冲突解决策略、手工修改保留策略以及在真实、多分支代码库中的可伸缩性与用户评估。

Abstract: Domain-Driven Design gives teams a shared vocabulary for complex business logic, but that vocabulary only stays useful as long as the model and the code agree with each other. In practice, they drift apart: code changes outpace the model, or model revisions never make it into the codebase. This paper presents JDomInO, a bidirectional synchronization toolchain for tactical DDD that keeps a Java codebase and its domain model connected through a shared metamodel, with the goal of keeping the two in sync as the system evolves. JDomInO generates Java code structure deterministically from a domain model (forward path) and reconstructs a domain model from existing Java code (reverse path). The forward path has been fully validated on a Hotel Management scenario covering all 12 building block types in the metamodel; the reverse path's mapping logic has passed unit testing, with end-to-end validation underway. We also outline how the structured domain model produced by JDomInO could serve as a precision context layer for AI code assistants, helping them respect aggregate boundaries and DDD semantics that raw source code alone does not convey.

</details>


### [110] [Agent-Based Test Assertion Generation via Diverse Perspective Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05822)
*Dong Wang,Qiaoyu Han,Lin Yang,Jianyi Zhou,Guangtai Liang,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AssertMate通过静态分析、三类生成代理与LLM裁判聚合不同视角，显著提高了断言生成的成功率与缺陷检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有断言生成方法（如ChatAssert）在准确率、依赖过采样与对一拍式提示的随机性敏感等方面表现不足，需更可靠且多样化的生成策略。

Method: 提出三段式框架：1) 实际值构建（静态分析+类型感知启发式）；2) 多视角期望值预测（代码生成、RAG检索增强生成、CoT推理多代理）；3) LLM作为裁判的协作选择机制以决定最终断言。

Result: 在Defects4J基准上，AssertMate在可编译性、通过率和缺陷检测能力上显著优于最先进方法；与EvoSuite结合时，在变异测试覆盖率与kill数上也有明显提升；消融研究表明三部分各自均有重要贡献。

Conclusion: AssertMate通过聚合多样化视角并引入代理式协作机制，显著提升了基于大模型的单元测试断言生成质量与可靠性，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Test assertions are critical elements of unit tests, serving as checkpoints to validate expected behavior and ensure software correctness. Numerous techniques have been proposed to automate assertion generation, with recent progress notably driven by large language models (LLMs). Despite the promise, existing approaches such as ChatAssert suffer from modest accuracy, heavy reliance on oversampling, and vulnerability to model randomness due to one-shot prompting. To address these limitations, we propose AssertMate, a novel agent-based assertion generation framework that enhances the quality and reliability of LLM-generated assertions through three key components: (1) actual value construction that identifies assertion targets via static analysis and type-aware heuristics; (2) multi-perspective expected value prediction using code generation, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning agents; and (3) an LLM-as-a-Judge collaboration mechanism to select the most appropriate assertion. Evaluation on the Defects4J benchmark demonstrates that AssertMate significantly outperforms state-of-the-art techniques in compilation success and pass rates, along with substantially higher bug detection capabilities. Integration with EvoSuite further validates AssertMate's practicality, yielding superior mutation coverage and kill counts. Ablation studies reveal that each of the three components makes a significant and complementary contribution to the overall performance. This work affirms the great potential of aggregating diverse perspectives to enhance the effectiveness of LLM-based assertion generation.

</details>


### [111] [CodeGrep: An RL-Trained Retrieval Agent for LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05886)
*Wuya Chen,Yihao yang,Yang Cao,Yue Lin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 代码代理中文件定位效率低的问题，构建并训练了一个 14B 的检索代理（CodeGrep），通过并行化文件检索大幅降低 token 与轮次开销，同时维持或略升解决率；检索精确率高低决定能否获益，且在优势层注入效率信号可减小策略漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 LLM 编码代理在定位需要补丁的文件上消耗大量 token（grep、glob、view_file 等探索调用），希望通过专门的检索代理把“找文件”这部分工作高效化，从而把 token 预算留给真正的补丁生成。

Method: 端到端用 GRPO 训练 14B 检索代理，令其以多轮并行方式调用 grep/glob/read 工具，返回候选文件给被冻结的下游编码模型。监督来自用 CATM 从 67K 轨迹中挖掘的数据；环境为 Git-worktree 多轮 RL 环境。效率信号在优势层（advantage）施加以减小 KL drift，从而更好地转化为下游效率提升。

Result: CodeGrep 是一个 14B 的检索代理，通过 GRPO 端到端训练，能够并行发起多轮 grep/glob/read 工具调用并返回候选文件给被冻结的下游编码代理。在 500 个 SWE-Bench Verified 实例上，在保持甚至略微提升解决率的同时（27.0% vs 基线 25.8%），在已解决实例上减少了 15% 的轮次和 19% 的 token 消耗。不同检索器的下游效用随精确率变化：BM25（0.375）会降级代理，Jina（0.445）中性，CodeGrep（0.677）超过阈值从而降低 rollout 成本。为了训练和评估，作者从 67K 开源代理轨迹中用 CATM 挖掘监督信号，并构建了基于 Git-worktree 的多轮代理 RL 环境；在优势层引入效率信号而非奖励层以减少 KL 偏移并将效率收益传递到下游。作者计划开源模型、训练流水线、RL 环境和评测工具。

Conclusion: 专门的文件检索代理可以显著提升 LLM 编程工作流的效率，但关键在于检索器的精确率与训练/部署成本；该方法在受控基准上效果明确，需进一步评估可推广性、实时延迟、训练成本和对更大代码库的适应性。

Abstract: Modern LLM coding agents such as Claude Code and OpenHands share a common inefficiency: they spend much of their token budget finding the file to patch, rather than patching it. On SWE-Bench Verified, a 30B OpenHands agent averages 23 rounds and 631K tokens per resolved issue, with many calls spent on grep, glob, and view_file during repository exploration. We introduce CodeGrep, a 14B retrieval agent trained end-to-end with GRPO to issue multi-turn parallel grep, glob, and read tool calls and return candidate files to a frozen downstream coding agent.
  On all 500 SWE-Bench Verified instances, CodeGrep preserves resolve rate while substantially improving efficiency: 27.0% versus 25.8% for the no-retrieval baseline, with 15% fewer rounds and 19% fewer tokens on resolved instances. Across retrievers, downstream utility follows a precision threshold: BM25 with precision 0.375 degrades the agent, Jina with precision 0.445 is neutral, and CodeGrep with precision 0.677 crosses the threshold at which retrieval begins to reduce rollout cost. To enable this study, we mine supervision from 67K open-source agent trajectories using CATM and build a Git-worktree environment for multi-turn agent RL. In our setting, applying the efficiency signal at the advantage layer rather than the reward layer reduces KL drift and translates cleanly into downstream efficiency. We will release the model, training pipeline, RL environment, and evaluation harnesses.

</details>


### [112] [Mapping the Emerging Curriculum for AI-Assisted Software Engineering via Syllabus Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05898)
*Francis Geng,Anshul Shah,Mia Chen,Paul Denny,Juho Leinonen,Bill Griswold,Gerald Soosai Raj,Leo Porter*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究通过对23份高校生成式AI与软件工程相关课程大纲的定性编码，概括出这些课程的目标、评估方式、主题与使用工具，发现了课程间的共性与差异，并给出课程设计建议与研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Generative AI coding tools are changing professional software development, prompting universities to create courses preparing students for AI-assisted workflows; analyzing course syllabi can provide empirical evidence about how this curricular area is defined and inform future curriculum design.

Method: 选取满足条件（上层课程、学分、公开可得、明确涉及生成式AI与软件工程）的23份大纲与课程材料，采用迭代定性编码法对学习目标、考核方式、主题目录与所示AI工具进行编码与归类，比较课程之间的共性与差异并总结设计启示。

Result: Analysis of 23 publicly available, upper-division, credit-bearing course syllabi that explicitly address Generative AI in software engineering, revealing common learning objectives, assessment types, topical coverage, and documented AI tools; identified shared patterns and differences across courses that can guide future course development.

Conclusion: 当前一批高校课程在训练学生使用生成式AI工具、理解其局限与伦理影响、以及将其整合进软件工程流程方面已有共识，但在深度、工具依赖、评估策略和理论/实践比例上存在显著差异；未来课程应平衡实践与理论、包含评估方法与伦理教育，并考虑持续更新教学材料以跟上技术演进。

Abstract: As Generative AI coding tools reshape professional software development, universities have begun designing courses to prepare students for AI-assisted development workflows. By analyzing the syllabi of these courses, we can gather empirical evidence about these courses, reveal how this emerging curricular area is being defined, and gain guidance for future curriculum design. We analyzed 23 publicly available syllabi and course materials of upper-division, credit-bearing courses that meet specific criteria, including explicitly addressing Generative AI in software engineering. Through iterative qualitative coding, we characterized courses' learning objectives, assessments, topics, and documented AI tools. Our analysis reveals commonalities and differences among these courses that allow researchers and educators to study and develop future courses.

</details>


### [113] [Escaping the Self-Repair Trap: Improving Test Oracle Generation via Dual-Context Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05917)
*Kefan Li,Hongyue Yu,Yuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 作者发现迭代自修可能导致生成容易被满足但不具缺陷揭露能力的断言（Self-Repair Trap），并提出非迭代的DCAware，通过高质量上下文（静态+选择性动态）替代多轮修复，以更有效地生成回归oracle，实验显示其在缺陷揭露能力与计算效率上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有基于修复的回归断言（oracle）生成方法存在的偏差问题：以执行成功率为优化目标的迭代自修复和基于执行反馈的策略，可能推动模型生成更容易满足但不具备揭露缺陷能力的断言，从而降低断言作为回归测试oracle的实际效用（即“Self-Repair Trap”）。作者希望提出一种替代策略，避免迭代反馈带来的退化，通过更高质量的上下文来提高断言的缺陷揭露能力。

Method: 设计DCAware框架：1）构建结构化静态上下文（如代码片段、API契约、测试前置条件等）并明确呈现给LLM；2）以策略化方式检索少量、相关的动态状态（如运行时变量、最近失败信息），而非多轮执行反馈；3）在单次推理中生成断言，避免迭代自修循环。通过执行测试与变异测试对比评估其缺陷揭露能力和执行成功率，并与迭代自修方法比较计算成本和效果。

Result: 提出了DCAware框架——一种非迭代、计算高效的方法，结合结构化的静态上下文和有选择的动态状态检索，以提高断言的信噪比。基于执行和变异测试的大量实验表明，DCAware在提高缺陷揭露效果的同时维持高执行成功率，性能优于以迭代自修为主的既有方法且计算成本明显更低。

Conclusion: 在回归oracle生成场景下，提高上下文质量（结构化静态信息与选择性动态状态）比增加迭代自修复杂性更能提升断言的缺陷揭露能力。DCAware以较低计算成本避免了自修陷阱，表明基于上下文的单轮生成是更稳健的策略。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for regression-oracle completion, where a test prefix is given and the current program version is treated as expected behavior. Recent approaches increasingly rely on iterative self-repair and execution feedback, but optimizing execution success does not necessarily yield strong fault-revealing oracles. This objective, widely adopted in repair-based methods, serves only as a proxy and may be misaligned with the true goal of oracle generation. Such misalignment biases the repair process, giving rise to a feedback-driven degeneration that we term the Self-Repair Trap, where iterative repair progressively drives models toward assertions that are easier to satisfy but less effective at detecting faults. To address this issue, we propose DCAware, a computationally efficient, non-iterative framework that prioritizes high signal-to-noise contextual grounding over multi-round repair. DCAware integrates structured static context with selectively retrieved dynamic states, enabling precise and robust oracle generation without iterative feedback loops. Extensive experiments based on execution and mutation testing show that DCAware consistently improves fault-revealing effectiveness while maintaining high execution success, outperforming prior methods with substantially lower computational cost. Our results suggest that improving contextual quality is more effective than adding iterative repair complexity in the studied regression-oracle setting.

</details>


### [114] [Expertise-Based Developer Assignment for Long-Term Software Components in Open-Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05919)
*Faheem Ullah,Babar Shah,William Shanks,Ayesha Mohsin,Abrar Ullah*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 利用开发者历史Git提交建模专长并自动为新项目推荐组件负责人；缓存显著加速响应；前10命中率为72.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是自动把合适的开发者分配到新项目中的长期、按组件划分的任务上，解决现有方法多针对短期或特定任务分配而忽视长期组件专长匹配的问题，从而提高开发效率与代码可维护性。

Method: 开发了一个基于历史Git提交的开发者专长建模器，并将其封装为Web应用。系统从开发者的提交记录中抽取特征（如修改文件路径、提交频率、语言/组件分布等），使用可替换的后端模型（可能包含基于特征的相似度或机器学习排序模型）计算每个新项目任务与候选开发者的匹配得分，输出排序候选列表。实现中加入服务器端数据缓存以加速重复查询，并对响应时间与推荐效果进行实验评估。

Result: 实现了可运行的Web应用；服务器端缓存将最差响应时间提升约9.86倍；在实验中，72.4%的任务在候选前10名中包含目标开发者（候选池上限为47）；系统响应速度受所选后端模型影响。

Conclusion: 方法证明可行且具实用价值：能在多数案例中把目标开发者排到候选前列，且通过缓存改善响应性能。但性能受后端模型限制，未来需在模型精度、扩展性、数据泛化和隐私保护上进一步加强。

Abstract: Open-source software development through GitHub has enabled countless software projects to be developed by developers from across the world. Assigning the right task to the right developer enables teams to work efficiently. Assigning a task which is not representative of a developer's expertise results in slower completion time and less maintainable code. Whilst much work has been done to automate this assignment for specific jobs, there is little work addressing the assignment of developers to expertise-specific long-term components of a new project. This paper produces a web application that models developers' expertise based on their previous Git commits and automatically assigns them to an optimal task within a new project. Testing showed that the system's speed varies depending on the back-end model used but server-side data caching improved worst-case speeds by a factor of 9.86. 72.4% of tasks had their target developer listed in the top 10, out of a possible 47.

</details>


### [115] [Sensor-Level Fault Diagnosis for Automotive Software Validation Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05921)
*Mohammad Abboush,Hamza Ouarrad,Andreas Rausch*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过先筛选违例再用统计/关系/语境描述符+指令微调LLM（4-bit LoRA）进行诊断，能在低标签量和单卡资源下实现可解释且高效的HIL故障定位；性能取决于收敛性而非单纯参数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HIL测试产生海量多变量传感器数据，人工审查不可行；阈值工具只能标记偏差但不能诊断；基于数据的分类器虽准确但需要大量标注且决策不透明，与ISO 26262的可追溯性要求存在冲突。研究旨在寻找既省标签又可解释、能嵌入验证环路的诊断方法。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：1) 在dSPACE实时平台上进行自动化需求检查以筛选违例录音；2) 对违例片段按滑动窗提取统计、关系、语境描述符，并将这些描述符装入固定提示(prompt)，对开放源指令微调LLM进行4-bit低秩适配(LoRA样式)以输出故障位置与文字化论证。对4个模型族（2B–8B参数）进行微调与评测，使用涵盖6类注入故障的汽油机案例。

Result: 最小模型在该任务上达成81.6%准确率，与最大模型持平；另一个相近规模模型未收敛，表明诊断能力更依赖于任务特化时的收敛性而非参数规模。全流程可在单个商用加速器上完成。

Conclusion: 在HIL预量产验证场景中，经任务特化适配的开放式指令微调大型语言模型可作为一种数据高效、可解释的故障检测与定位引擎；在所测用例中，小模型在收敛条件下可达到与更大模型相当的诊断精度。

Abstract: The pre-series validation of automotive software on hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) platforms produces large volumes of multivariate sensor recordings whose assessment against functional safety requirements exceeds what manual review can sustain at campaign scale. Threshold-based tooling reports that a deviation has occurred but neither identifies its nature nor locates its source, while data-driven classifiers, although accurate, rely on large labelled datasets and return opaque decisions that sit uneasily with the traceability demanded by ISO 26262. This study examines whether open-source instruction-tuned large language models (LLMs), given a textual description of sensor behaviour, can serve as data-efficient and interpretable engines for fault detection and diagnosis inside the validation loop. A two-phase framework is proposed: automated requirement checking on a dSPACE real-time platform first isolates the recordings that violate a safety requirement, and only these are inspected, with sliding windows of the signals reduced to statistical, relational, and contextual descriptors, embedded in a fixed prompt, and mapped by a 4-bit low-rank-adapted LLM to a fault location accompanied by a written justification. Four model families ranging from two to eight billion parameters were adapted and tested on a gasoline-engine case study spanning six injected fault classes. The smallest model matched the largest at 81.6\% accuracy, whereas a comparably sized model failed to converge, indicating that diagnostic competence under task-specific adaptation follows convergence rather than parameter count, with the entire adapt-and-evaluate cycle fitting on a single commodity accelerator.

</details>


### [116] [AgentExecutor: Partial Code Execution via Agentic Context Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05959)
*Junkai Chen,Chengran Yang,Xing Hu,Zhenhao Li,Xin Xia,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AgentExecutor uses multiple LLM agents in a three-phase process to more flexibly and efficiently execute partial code by expanding actions and introducing adaptive optimizations, yielding substantially better coverage, speed, and cost than prior methods.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Partial code execution is hard due to missing context and dependencies; prior LLM-based methods (e.g., LExecutor, Treefix) are limited by small action spaces, weak feedback mechanisms, and inflexible optimization, reducing effectiveness and efficiency.

Method: AgentExecutor: multi-agent LLM framework with three-phase pipeline—execution environment preparation, dynamic exploration with iterative refinement, and prefix evolution via program synthesis. Agents perform iterative think-act-feedback loops, manipulate richer action space (create files, configure env) and apply adaptive optimization (coverage-guided context pruning, prefix evolution).

Result: On two datasets (Stack Overflow snippets and open-source project code), AgentExecutor achieves up to 94% and 90% code coverage, improving over Treefix by 19.9% and 13.8%. It also cuts execution time by up to 80.3% and cost by up to 56.6%. Demonstrates improved execution quality and efficiency.

Conclusion: AgentExecutor is an effective and efficient approach for partial code execution, enabled by multi-agent exploration and adaptive optimization; empirical results show strong improvements in coverage, runtime, and cost over state-of-the-art baselines.

Abstract: Executing code snippets is essential for dynamic program analysis, but it remains challenging to execute an arbitrary code snippet due to issues like missing context and incomplete dependencies. Existing approaches to partial code execution, such as LExecutor and Treefix, leverage the power of language models to infer missing information and enable execution. However, they suffer from (i) limited action spaces and feedback, and (ii) rigid optimization strategies, which restrict their effectiveness and efficiency. In this paper, we propose AgentExecutor, a novel multi-agent framework for partial code execution. Our approach introduces a three-phase design: execution environment preparation, dynamic exploration with iterative refinement, and prefix evolution via program synthesis. Supported by the power of LLM agents who can think, act, and get feedback iteratively, AgentExecutor is able to autonomously explore a richer action space, enabling diverse operations such as creating resource files and resolving environment configuration. Furthermore, it adopts adaptive optimization strategies, including coverage-guided context pruning and prefix evolution via program synthesis, to systematically improve the execution quality of partial code. We evaluate AgentExecutor on two widely used datasets comprising Stack Overflow snippets and open-source project code. The results show that AgentExecutor achieves up to 94% and 90% code coverage, outperforming the state-of-the-art approach Treefix by 19.9% and 13.8%, respectively. In addition, AgentExecutor significantly reduces execution time (by up to 80.3%) and cost (by up to 56.6%). These findings demonstrate that AgentExecutor provides an effective and efficient solution for partial code execution.

</details>


### [117] [LangChoiceBench: Measuring and Explaining Programming-Language Choice in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06041)
*Lukas Twist,Twm Stone,Helen Yannakoudakis,Jie M. Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 作者提出了LangChoiceBench，一个衡量模型在项目级代码生成时是否偏好Python及相关行为的基准。覆盖28个以Python为不适合默认语言的项目领域，评估25个LLM，并分析9,826条推理轨迹。主要发现：模型过度选择Python、推荐与实现不一致、小型模型更偏好Python并缺乏语言多样性，存在“幻影证据”和自相矛盾的推理输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏系统性方法去量化和比较不同模型在项目级代码生成中对Python的偏好以及该偏好是否合理，研究旨在填补这一评估空白并揭示相关失败模式。

Method: 构建LangChoiceBench：挑选28个跨七个软件领域的项目（这些领域中Python通常不是优先选择），为每个项目设计评估场景，测量三类指标：Python偏好（选择率）、推荐—实现一致性、语言多样性；对25个不同LLM进行生成评估；对9,826条模型推理轨迹进行定量/定性分析以识别驱动因素和失败模式（如幻影证据、推理与代码不一致）。

Result: 实证结果显示：1) Python被显著过度选择；2) 推荐语言和生成实现之间一致性低；3) 较小的开源模型更倾向于选择Python并表现出更低的语言多样性；4) 多数选择由便利性或自动化驱动，少数情况存在幻影证据或推理/实现自相矛盾。

Conclusion: LLM在项目级代码生成中普遍存在不恰当的Python偏好，这种偏好常由自动化或便利性驱动，且会产生不一致或虚构的支持理由，需要通过更好的评估基准和定向干预来缓解。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been shown to exhibit strong Python preferences when generating project-level code, but there is currently no systematic way to measure this behaviour across new models. To bridge this gap, we introduce LangChoiceBench, a project-level code-generation benchmark for measuring Python preference, recommendation-implementation consistency, and language diversity. LangChoiceBench covers 28 projects across seven software areas where Python is often a poor default. We evaluate 25 diverse LLMs and find that Python remains heavily over-selected, recommendation-implementation consistency is low, and smaller open-weight models generally show stronger Python preference and lower language diversity. We further analyse 9,826 reasoning traces and find that most Python choices are automatic or driven primarily by ease, rather than explicit consideration of project requirements. In a smaller but important set of cases, models fabricate contextual support for choosing Python - a failure mode we call phantom evidence - or produce code that contradicts the language selected in their own reasoning.

</details>


### [118] [In Terms of Explainability: Refining Requirements for Self-Explainable Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06049)
*Arno Leue,Akhila Bairy,Maike Schwammberger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出统一可解释性定义与分层需求框架，强调解释质量，旨在推动可解释系统的标准化与可审计化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主与软件密集系统复杂度上升，透明性/可解释性需求增加；现有缺乏普适定义与开发指南，法规（如EU AI Act）和标准（IEEE 7001-2021）已提示需要统一标准。

Method: 分析并整合现有可解释性定义，构建统一定义与结构化需求（分类法），并提出将解释的正确性/质量纳入定义以支持标准化与认证。

Result: 输出统一的可解释性定义、结构化的可解释性需求分类法，并建议包含解释质量判断，为制定正式标准、认证与审计提供依据。

Conclusion: 统一词汇与需求框架为标准化和审计铺路，但需进一步形式化、量化解释质量、提供实证验证与工具支撑，以便在不同领域和复杂系统中可行并可被采纳。

Abstract: Autonomous and software-intensive systems have been growing in occurrence, complexity, and assumed responsibility. Due to the high complexity of these systems, properties like transparency and explainability must be a focus of investigation. To date, no universally applicable definition and guide for the development of (self-)explainable systems exists. A need for explainability standards has already been recognized in the EU AI Act and the IEEE Transparency Standard 7001-2021. To address this need, we propose unified definitions in explainability based on an analysis and combination of existing definitions. Additionally, we present structured explainability requirements that are necessary to build (self-)explainable systems. By analysing the resulting taxonomy, we propose the incorporation of explanation goodness and thus correctness of explanations into the unified definitions. With our approach, we support the development of formal standards for (self-)explainable systems. Establishing such a uniform taxonomy also is a beneficial step towards certifying or auditing explainable systems.

</details>


### [119] [DCAS: Decoupling CLI Agent Scaffolding to Internalize Planning across Scaffolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06113)
*Kishanthan Thangarajah,Boyuan Chen,Ahmed E. Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Fine-tuning on OpenHands induces scaffold-specific planning behaviors that harm cross-scaffold generalization. DCAS (a backend-substitution proxy) enables cross-scaffold evaluation and collecting planning-aware trajectories; training on these trajectories improves generalization and shows planning (explicit vs implicit) is separable and high-leverage.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLI-based software-engineering agents are increasingly trained on trajectory datasets collected under a single scaffold (OpenHands), causing models to overfit to scaffold-specific conventions and fail when deployed under other execution scaffolds. The goal is to decouple planning from fixed scaffold artifacts and make planning a learned capability to improve cross-scaffold robustness.

Method: Introduce DCAS proxy to transparently mediate API traffic between any CLI scaffold and backend model; perform controlled interventions swapping plan source to measure planning quality effect; collect a small set of planning-aware trajectories through DCAS under a single scaffold; fine-tune models on these trajectories and evaluate across multiple non-training scaffolds; perform analyses to separate explicit planning artifacts vs implicit execution conventions in training data.

Result: Paper identifies scaffold-specific overfitting when fine-tuning CLI agents on OpenHands trajectories: models perform well under training scaffold but degrade on other scaffolds; untrained bases don't diverge, so fine-tuning induces scaffold-bound behaviors. Hypothesis: planning structure (explicit pre-execution plans and implicit execution conventions) is the main scaffold-specific factor. Contribution: DCAS, an interception layer that proxies API calls between arbitrary scaffold and backend model, enabling cross-scaffold evaluation and planning-aware trajectory collection. Empirical findings: plan-source intervention shows planning quality has large impact exceeding observed cross-scaffold drops; fine-tuning on a small set of DCAS-collected planning-aware trajectories yields consistent gains across non-training scaffolds; explicit and implicit planning are separable in data.

Conclusion: Scaffold-specific planning conventions, not base model limitations, drive cross-scaffold performance drops; turning planning into a learned model capability via planner-aware trajectory collection (enabled by DCAS) improves robustness across scaffolds. Explicit and implicit planning can be disentangled and targeted in fine-tuning to reduce overfitting to a single training scaffold.

Abstract: CLI-based software-engineering agents have matured rapidly, yet the open ecosystem has converged on a single training environment: trajectory datasets used to fine-tune open models are collected almost exclusively under OpenHands. Models fine-tuned on this data score well under OpenHands but degrade substantially when deployed under any non-training scaffold. Untrained base models do not show this divergence, indicating the gap is fine-tuning-induced and tied to the conventions of the training scaffold. We argue that a load-bearing scaffold-specific behavior is planning structure, in two senses this paper distinguishes: explicit planning, a pre-execution plan produced as a first-class artifact, and implicit planning, the structural conventions that shape execution throughout the agent loop. Under this hypothesis, closing the gap requires moving planning from a fixed scaffold artifact to a learned model capability. We introduce Decoupling CLI Agent Scaffolding (DCAS), a backend-substitution interception layer that routes API traffic between any CLI scaffold and any backend model without modifying the scaffold, enabling cross-scaffold evaluation and planning-aware trajectory collection. Using DCAS, a controlled plan-source intervention confirms planning quality is a high-leverage component, with gains exceeding the cross-scaffold drops we observe. A model fine-tuned on a small set of DCAS-collected planning-aware trajectories under a single scaffold gains consistently across non-training scaffolds, and the two senses of planning are empirically separable in training data.

</details>


### [120] [Learning Globally Reusable Skills for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06153)
*Chen Yang,Jiashuo Tian,Ziqi Wang,Xinyin Liu,Meiru Ye,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: GSE 提出通过维护技能关系图并结合聚类整合与回放校验，使技能进化从局部更新走向全局协同，从而提升技能的兼容性与泛化能力，在多个编码代理与软件工程任务上显著提高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化技能进化多为串行局部更新，忽视技能间的相互关系且容易导致对单一任务过拟合，因而缺乏跨任务泛化能力；有必要引入全局视角与验证机制以保证技能库一致性与可复用性，从而持续提升 LLM 代理在实际任务中的表现。

Method: 1) Skill Relation Graph (SRG)：显式建模技能节点与边，用以联合优化技能兼容性并在进化中协同更新；2) Cluster-based Skill Consolidation：将局部更新抽象为聚类中心，整合为更通用的技能原型以提升泛化；3) Replay-driven Verification：用历史样本或回放数据检验新技能以防止过拟合与行为回退；整体流程为：局部更新→在 SRG 中联合调整→基于聚类合并抽象→回放验证并择优更新技能库。

Result: GSE 在两个软件工程任务（揭示漏洞的测试生成、过滤误报的缺陷报告）和两种编码代理（OpenHands、mini-SWE-agent）上取得了显著提升：precision/recall/F1 均优于现有进化方法；相对改进在测试生成任务上 precision/recall 分别为 6.1%~34.1% 与 31.8%~180.0%，在误报过滤上分别为 15.4%~96.4% 与 13.1%~19.8%；内部工业代理部署带来 61.4% 的 F1 提升。

Conclusion: GSE 有效缓解了现有技能进化中忽视技能间关系与局部过拟合的问题：通过显式建模技能关系并进行簇级整合与回放验证，既保持了技能库的一致性，又提取了可复用能力，实验显示其在多种设置下均显著优于对比方法，且具有良好工业适配性。

Abstract: Automated skill evolution enables Large Language Model (LLM) agents to continuously improve without expensive retraining. However, existing approaches typically treat skill evolution as a sequence of local updates, overlooking relationships among skills and often producing overfitted skill updates that fail to generalize across tasks. We propose GSE, a globalized skill evolution framework that jointly optimizes skill compatibility and skill generalization. To preserve consistency across the skill bank, GSE maintains a Skill Relation Graph (SRG) that explicitly models and co-evolves inter-skill relationships. To improve generalization, GSE performs cluster-based skill consolidation to abstract reusable capabilities from local updates and employs replay-driven verification to prevent overfitting and behavioral regressions. We evaluate GSE on two representative software engineering tasks: bug-revealing test generation and false-positive bug report filtering. Across two state-of-the-art coding agents, OpenHands and mini-SWE-agent, GSE consistently achieves the best precision, recall, and F1-score. Compared with existing evolution techniques, GSE improves precision and recall by 6.1%~34.1% and 31.8%~180.0% for test generation, and by 15.4%~96.4% and 13.1%~19.8% for false-positive filtering. Deployment on an internal industrial agent further yields a 61.4% improvement in F1-score, demonstrating the effectiveness and generalizability of GSE for evolving effective skills.

</details>


### [121] [Predicting Agile Success: The Critical Few Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06228)
*Ridewaan Hanslo,Maureen Tanner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 采用实证的实证主义方法，使用SmartPLS的PLS-SEM分析从敏捷从业者处收集的数据，识别出若干显著影响项目信息成功的CSF，进而构建了一个可用于项目成功评估的新模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别敏捷软件开发项目成功的关键成功因素（CSF），并将项目严格评估于当代项目成功标准之下，以解释为何尽管敏捷项目较传统方法更成功，但整体成功率仍偏低。

Method: 研究采用实证主义范式，使用PLS-SEM（通过SmartPLS软件）分析问卷数据以检验构念间关系并识别显著的关键成功因素。

Result: 研究发现若干关键因素显著影响敏捷项目成功，并提出了一个新的模型用于评估和衡量项目成功。

Conclusion: 所提出的模型可作为评估敏捷项目成功的工具，但论文需在方法细节、测量信度与效度、样本与外部有效性等方面给予更多信息以验证其普适性与可复现性。

Abstract: While Agile projects are more successful than traditional software development project management approaches, their overall success rate remains relatively low, with a high percentage of projects still deemed challenged or failed. This low project success rate is attributed to projects not being rigorously evaluated against critical success factors (CSF) of Agile projects and contemporary project success criteria. To identify the CSF that contribute to Agile software development project success as perceived by Agile practitioners, this study used a positivist approach to investigate the CSF of Agile software development projects. PLS-SEM with SmartPLS was used to analyse the data and test the hypotheses to identify significant relationships between the constructs and project success criteria. This research found that a few critical factors significantly contribute to Agile project success. The study developed a novel model that can be used to evaluate and measure project success.

</details>


### [122] [Automatic Translation of Unstructured Requirements into Linear Temporal Logic through Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06287)
*Alexandra Newcomb,Omar Ochoa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 现代通用LLM在无需微调的情况下，能在少样本提示下为非结构化需求生成有用的LTL公式，但尚需人工复核与更大规模/严格评估以用于安全关键场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将无结构自然语言需求自动转为数学精确定义（如LTL）对安全/任务关键系统的验证至关重要，但手工形式化费时且易错，故评估通用LLM作为自动化/半自动化工具的可行性。

Method: 对6个现代LLM使用少样本提示（few-shot），在15条异构需求上每对生成5次，共450个LTL候选。评估包括人工语义判断、pass@1/3/5统计与语法自一致性度量；并为生成公式配自然语言解释与时间线可视化以评估可理解性。

Result: 作者对6个通用LLM在15条结构各异的自然语言需求上进行少样本提示测试，收集每对5次生成，总共450条LTL候选。用手工语义评估、pass@k（k=1,3,5）和自一致性度量评估。结论是：无需特定微调，现代通用LLM在NL→LTL任务上已达到实用性水平，并可通过模型生成的自然语言解释和时间线可视化改善可理解性，从而成为半自动形式化流程的前端助手。

Conclusion: LLM能作为半自动形式化的前端辅助工具，但不能替代专家：需要更大样本、自动化语义验证、领域微调、基于模型检查的后验验证和人机交互流程才能在安全关键系统中广泛部署。

Abstract: Automatically translating unstructured natural language requirements into formal specifications remains a challenge in requirements engineering and formal methods, particularly for safety- and mission-critical systems whose verification depends on mathematically precise specifications. This paper evaluates whether contemporary off-the-shelf Large Language Models (LLMs) can help bridge this gap by generating Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) formulas directly from unstructured requirements. The study examines six modern LLMs using a few-shot prompting strategy on a heterogeneous benchmark of 15 structurally varied requirements. Five independent generations were collected for each requirement-model pair, yielding 450 candidate LTL formulas in total. Performance was assessed through manual semantic evaluation, pass@k for k in {1, 3, 5}, and a self-consistency measure capturing syntactic reproducibility across stochastic trials. The results indicate that current general-purpose LLMs can achieve practically significant performance on the unstructured NL-to-LTL task without task-specific fine-tuning. The study also considers understandability for non-experts by pairing generated formulas with model-produced natural language explanations and discussing the complementary use of timeline-based LTL visualization. The findings suggest that modern LLMs are becoming viable front-end assistants for semi-automated formalization workflows.

</details>
